- **crunchflow-analyst**: CrunchFlow stage 1 — turns a one-line idea into a concrete requirements brief (docs/01-brief.md). Research and analysis only; forbidden from writing any code. ([ahamedumar15/crunchflow](https://github.com/ahamedumar15/crunchflow/blob/HEAD/crunchflow/agents/analyst.md))
- **crunchflow-architect**: CrunchFlow stage 2 — one of three competing architects. - **crunchflow-planner**: CrunchFlow stage 4 — shards the approved spec into small tasks with test criteria and an ALLOWED FILES list per task (the scope guard enforces this list). Cannot write code. - **crunchflow-qa**: CrunchFlow stage 6 — adversarial QA that trusts nobody. ([ahamedumar15/crunchflow](https://github.com/ahamedumar15/crunchflow/blob/HEAD/crunchflow/agents/qa.md))

- **cs-deep-research**: Rigor-first meta-research persona for high-stakes questions.
